17: class MetadataCacheStore:
18:     def __init__(self, cache_path: Path):
19:         self._cache_path = cache_path
20:         self._lock = threading.Lock()
21:         self._ensure_db()
22: 
23:     def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
24:         return sqlite3.connect(self._cache_path, timeout=5)
25: 
26:     def _ensure_db(self) -> None:
27:         self._cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
28:         with self._connect() as conn:
29:             conn.execute(
30:                 """
31:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata_cache (
32:                     cache_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
33:                     db_fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL,
34:                     operation TEXT NOT NULL,
35:                     args_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
36:                     value_json TEXT NOT NULL,
37:                     created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
38:                     expires_at INTEGER
39:                 )
40:                 """
41:             )
42:             conn.execute(
43:                 """
44:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metadata_cache_db_operation
45:                 ON metadata_cache (db_fingerprint, operation)
46:                 """
47:             )
48:             conn.execute(
49:                 """
50:                 CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_metadata_cache_expires
51:                 ON metadata_cache (expires_at)
52:                 """
53:             )
54: 
55:     def get(self, cache_key: str) -> Any | None:
56:         with self._lock:
57:             with self._connect() as conn:
58:                 row = conn.execute(
59:                     "SELECT value_json, expires_at FROM metadata_cache WHERE cache_key = ?",
60:                     (cache_key,),
61:                 ).fetchone()
62:                 if row is None:
63:                     return None
64: 
65:                 value_json, expires_at = row
66:                 now = int(time.time())
67:                 if expires_at is not None and expires_at <= now:
68:                     conn.execute(
69:                         "DELETE FROM metadata_cache WHERE cache_key = ?", (cache_key,)
70:                     )
71:                     return None
72: 
73:                 return json.loads(value_json)
74: 
75:     def set(
76:         self,
77:         *,
78:         cache_key: str,
79:         db_fingerprint: str,
80:         operation: str,
81:         args_hash: str,
82:         value: Any,
83:         ttl_seconds: int | None,
84:     ) -> None:
85:         now = int(time.time())
86:         expires_at = (
87:             now + ttl_seconds if ttl_seconds is not None and ttl_seconds > 0 else None
88:         )
89: 
90:         with self._lock:
91:             with self._connect() as conn:
92:                 conn.execute(
93:                     """
94:                     INSERT INTO metadata_cache (
95:                         cache_key,
96:                         db_fingerprint,
97:                         operation,
98:                         args_hash,
99:                         value_json,
100:                         created_at,
101:                         expires_at
102:                     ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
103:                     ON CONFLICT(cache_key) DO UPDATE SET
104:                         value_json=excluded.value_json,
105:                         created_at=excluded.created_at,
106:                         expires_at=excluded.expires_at
107:                     """,
108:                     (
109:                         cache_key,
110:                         db_fingerprint,
111:                         operation,
112:                         args_hash,
113:                         json.dumps(value),
114:                         now,
115:                         expires_at,
116:                     ),
117:                 )
118: 
119:     def invalidate(self, db_fingerprint: str | None = None) -> int:
120:         with self._lock:
121:             with self._connect() as conn:
122:                 if db_fingerprint is None:
123:                     cursor = conn.execute("DELETE FROM metadata_cache")
124:                 else:
125:                     cursor = conn.execute(
126:                         "DELETE FROM metadata_cache WHERE db_fingerprint = ?",
127:                         (db_fingerprint,),
128:                     )
129:                 return cursor.rowcount
130: 
131: 
132: def _hash(value: str) -> str:
133:     return hashlib.sha256(value.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
134: 
135: 
136: def _normalize_args(operation_args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
137:     payload = operation_args or {}
138:     return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
139: 
140: 
141: def get_db_fingerprint(database_uri: str | None = None) -> str:
142:     uri = database_uri or DBConfig().get_database_uri()
143:     return _hash(uri)
144: 
145: 
146: def build_cache_key(
147:     *,
148:     database_uri: str | None,
149:     operation: str,
150:     operation_args: dict[str, Any] | None,
151: ) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
152:     db_fingerprint = get_db_fingerprint(database_uri)
153:     normalized_args = _normalize_args(operation_args)
154:     args_hash = _hash(normalized_args)
155:     cache_key = _hash(f"{db_fingerprint}|{operation}|{args_hash}")
156:     return cache_key, db_fingerprint, args_hash
157: 
158: 
159: _cache_store = MetadataCacheStore(AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_PATH)
160: 
161: 
162: def get_cached_metadata(
163:     *,
164:     operation: str,
165:     operation_args: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
166:     database_uri: str | None = None,
167:     bypass_cache: bool = False,
168: ) -> Any | None:
169:     if not AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_ENABLED:
170:         return None
171: 
172:     if AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_BYPASS_DEFAULT or bypass_cache:
173:         logger.debug(f"metadata cache bypassed for operation={operation}")
174:         return None
175: 
176:     cache_key, _, _ = build_cache_key(
177:         database_uri=database_uri,
178:         operation=operation,
179:         operation_args=operation_args,
180:     )
181:     cached = _cache_store.get(cache_key)
182:     if cached is None:
183:         logger.debug(f"metadata cache miss for operation={operation}")
184:     else:
185:         logger.debug(f"metadata cache hit for operation={operation}")
186:     return cached
187: 
188: 
189: def set_cached_metadata(
190:     *,
191:     operation: str,
192:     value: Any,
193:     operation_args: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
194:     database_uri: str | None = None,
195:     bypass_cache: bool = False,
196: ) -> None:
197:     if not AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_ENABLED:
198:         return
199: 
200:     if AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_BYPASS_DEFAULT or bypass_cache:
201:         logger.debug(
202:             f"metadata cache write skipped by bypass for operation={operation}"
203:         )
204:         return
205: 
206:     cache_key, db_fingerprint, args_hash = build_cache_key(
207:         database_uri=database_uri,
208:         operation=operation,
209:         operation_args=operation_args,
210:     )
211:     _cache_store.set(
212:         cache_key=cache_key,
213:         db_fingerprint=db_fingerprint,
214:         operation=operation,
215:         args_hash=args_hash,
216:         value=value,
217:         ttl_seconds=AppConfig().METADATA_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS,
218:     )
219: 
220: 
221: def invalidate_metadata_cache(database_uri: str | None = None) -> int:
222:     db_fingerprint = None if database_uri is None else get_db_fingerprint(database_uri)
223:     deleted = _cache_store.invalidate(db_fingerprint)
224:     target = "all databases" if db_fingerprint is None else "active database"
225:     logger.info(f"metadata cache invalidated for {target}; entries deleted={deleted}")
226:     return deleted

</content>

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 27.9s)

_Thinking:_



Good question.  
`local ST` = local **SentenceTransformers** model from HuggingFace (runs on your machine).

You do **not** embed everything on each agent run.
- Index phase: chunk schema/MDL/instructions/verified pairs -> embed once -> store vectors.
- Query phase (every question): embed user question once -> retrieve nearest context from 3 channels.
- Learning phase (only on confirmed success): embed that new NL->SQL pair once -> append.
- Re-index only when schema/MDL/chunking/embedding-model changes.

So cost pattern:
- Heavy work = indexing (occasional).
- Per request = one query embedding + vector search (light).

For laptop demo, best reliability setup:
- Local tiny CPU model as primary (`BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` or `intfloat/e5-small-v2`).
- Prebuild index on desktop, copy index files to laptop.
- Remote OpenRouter embedding as fallback only (free tier can rate-limit / be unstable).
- Keep lexical fallback if embedding unavailable.

Important: if embedding model/dimension changes, rebuild index. Old vectors incompatible.

One final lock before build:  
Use `EMBEDDING_MODE=hybrid` (local primary + OpenRouter fallback) as default? (recommended)

---

## User

cant i build the embeded db from my desktop with my local ST model and create an index there and just run inference with the laptop ?

---

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 29.1s)

_Thinking:_



Yes, can. Desktop build index once. Laptop use index for retrieval.

Key rule: query embedding must exist each question.
- No per-query reindex.
- Only embed user question at runtime.
- Then nearest-neighbor search in stored vectors.

Hard constraints:
- Same embedding space for docs + query.
- Same model id/version, dimension, normalization, prompt format.
- If index built with local ST model, query must use same model.  
  Different model -> retrieval quality collapse.

So:
- Desktop: chunk + embed schema/MDL/instructions/pairs -> save vector DB.
- Copy vector DB + metadata to laptop.
- Laptop: load DB, embed question, retrieve top-k, continue agent flow.

If laptop too weak:
1) Use tiny local model on both machines (`bge-small-en-v1.5` / `e5-small-v2`) (recommended).
2) Or use remote embeddings for both indexing + query.
3) Or keep lexical fallback when embed unavailable.

One lock: want strict single-model pipeline (same local model both machines), or dual-mode with fallback (local primary + remote backup)?

19: class ConnectionSession:
20:     """A database connection session with automatic cleanup.

23:     providing context manager support for guaranteed resource cleanup.

31:         # ... use session ...
32:         session.close()  # Must be called explicitly

110:         # Adjust config for tunnel if created
111:         if tunnel:
112:             connect_config = config.with_endpoint(host=host, port=str(port))
113:         else:
114:             connect_config = config
115: 
116:         # Get provider and connect
117:         provider_candidate = get_provider_fn(config.db_type)
118:         if isinstance(provider_candidate, DatabaseProvider):
119:             provider = provider_candidate
120:         elif hasattr(provider_candidate, "connection_factory"):
121:             provider = cast(DatabaseProvider, provider_candidate)
122:         else:
123:             adapter = provider_candidate
124:             spec = get_provider_spec(config.db_type)
125:             schema = get_provider_schema(config.db_type)
126:             provider = build_adapter_provider(spec, schema, adapter)
127:         connection = provider.connection_factory.connect(connect_config)
128:         try:
129:             provider.post_connect(connection, config)
130:         except Exception:
131:             pass
132: 
133:         return cls(connection, provider, config, tunnel)
134: 
135:     @property
136:     def connection(self) -> Any:
137:         """Get the raw database connection object."""
138:         return self._connection
139: 
140:     @property
141:     def provider(self) -> DatabaseProvider:
142:         """Get the database provider."""
143:         return self._provider
144: 
145:     @property
146:     def adapter(self) -> Any:
147:         """Compatibility alias for the underlying adapter."""
148:         return self._provider.connection_factory
149: 
150:     @property
151:     def config(self) -> ConnectionConfig:
152:         """Get the connection configuration."""
153:         return self._config
154: 
155:     @property
156:     def tunnel(self) -> Any | None:
157:         """Get the SSH tunnel object if present."""
158:         return self._tunnel
159: 
160:     @property
161:     def has_tunnel(self) -> bool:
162:         """Check if this session has an active SSH tunnel."""
163:         return self._tunnel is not None
164: 
165:     @property
166:     def is_closed(self) -> bool:
167:         """Check if this session has been closed."""
168:         return self._closed
169: 
170:     @property
171:     def executor(self) -> DatabaseExecutor:
172:         """Get or create the database executor for serialized operations.
173: 
174:         The executor is lazily created on first access. All database operations
175:         should go through this executor to ensure thread-safe access.
176: 
177:         Returns:
178:             The DatabaseExecutor for this session.
179: 
180:         Raises:
181:             RuntimeError: If the session has been closed.
182:         """
183:         if self._closed:
184:             raise RuntimeError("Cannot get executor for closed session")
185:         if self._executor is None:
186:             from .executor import DatabaseExecutor
187: 
188:             self._executor = DatabaseExecutor(self)
189:         return self._executor
190: 
191:     def switch_database(self, database: str) -> None:
192:         """Switch to a different database without recreating the session.
193: 
194:         This is used for databases like PostgreSQL that don't support
195:         cross-database queries. It closes the current connection and
196:         opens a new one to the specified database, reusing the SSH tunnel.
197: 
198:         Args:
199:             database: The database name to switch to.
200: 
201:         Raises:
202:             RuntimeError: If the session has been closed.
203:             Any database-specific connection errors.
204:         """
205:         if self._closed:
206:             raise RuntimeError("Cannot switch database on closed session")
207: 
208:         # Create new config with the database
209:         new_config = self._config.with_endpoint(database=database)
210: 
211:         # Determine connection config (use tunnel if present)
212:         if self._tunnel:
213:             # Reuse tunnel - get local bind address
214:             local_host, local_port = self._tunnel.local_bind_address
215:             connect_config = new_config.with_endpoint(
216:                 host=local_host, port=str(local_port)
217:             )
218:         else:
219:             connect_config = new_config
220: 
221:         # Close old connection (but keep tunnel)
222:         if self._connection is not None:
223:             try:
224:                 close_fn = getattr(self._connection, "close", None)
225:                 if callable(close_fn):
226:                     close_fn()
227:             except Exception:
228:                 pass
229: 
230:         # Open new connection
231:         self._connection = self._provider.connection_factory.connect(connect_config)
232:         self._config = new_config
233: 
234:     def close(self) -> None:
235:         """Close the session and release all resources.
236: 
237:         This method is idempotent - calling it multiple times is safe.
238:         It shuts down the executor first (to stop pending operations),
239:         then closes the database connection, then stops the SSH tunnel.
240:         Exceptions during cleanup are silently caught to ensure all
241:         resources are released.
242:         """
243:         if self._closed:
244:             return
245: 
246:         # Shutdown executor first (don't wait for pending operations)
247:         if self._executor is not None:
248:             try:
249:                 self._executor.shutdown(wait=False)
250:             except Exception:
251:                 pass
252:             self._executor = None
253: 
254:         # Close database connection
255:         if self._connection is not None:
256:             try:
257:                 close_fn = getattr(self._connection, "close", None)
258:                 if callable(close_fn):
259:                     close_fn()
260:             except Exception:
261:                 pass
262:             self._connection = None
263: 
264:         # Stop SSH tunnel
265:         if self._tunnel is not None:
266:             try:
267:                 self._tunnel.stop()
268:             except Exception:
269:                 pass
270:             self._tunnel = None
271: 
272:         self._closed = True
273: 
274:     def __enter__(self) -> ConnectionSession:
275:         """Enter the context manager."""
276:         return self
277: 
278:     def __exit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc_val: Any, exc_tb: Any) -> None:
279:         """Exit the context manager, ensuring cleanup."""
280:         self.close()
281: 
282:     def __del__(self) -> None:
283:         """Destructor to catch unclosed sessions (best-effort cleanup)."""
284:         if not self._closed:
285:             self.close()

(End of file - total 285 lines)
</content>

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 49.4s)

_Thinking:_



Good. Dual mode works.

Critical technical fact:
- Different embedding models = different vector space.
- Index built with local model cannot be queried well with remote model embeddings.
- So fallback needs policy, else recall quality breaks hard.

What happens in your indexing phase (with MDL+RAG) vs current flow:
- Current: LLM picks tables -> fetch full schema + 1 sample row -> generate SQL.
- New index phase: introspect DB baseline + load MDL YAML overrides -> merge semantic graph -> chunk docs (schema/instructions/memory) -> embed once -> persist.
- Query phase: embed question once -> retrieve top-k from 3 channels -> inject into prompt -> generate/validate/execute.
- No re-embedding whole DB each run. Only question embedding per run, plus pair embedding on confirmed success.

Recommended default policy for dual mode:
- Keep separate index per embedding profile (`local:<model>` and `remote:<model>`).
- Runtime fallback local->remote only if remote index already exists.
- If fallback index missing: use lexical fallback, no cross-model vector search.

One final lock before build (recommended = 1):
1) Separate per-profile indexes (recommended)  
2) Force remote query on local index (fast to code, bad quality risk)  
3) Disable remote fallback for retrieval, use lexical fallback only when local down

Use **local first**. Keep HF Inference as **backup**, not primary.

- Local embedding run better for demo. No internet/rate-limit/cold-start surprises.

- Desktop GPU use for fast indexing/reindex. Laptop use for normal retrieval runtime.

- HF free endpoint good as fallback path. Not good as only path for final demo reliability.
- Keep **profile-aware indexes**: same model/profile for index+query, else retrieval quality drops.

Recommended policy:
1. `EMBEDDING_MODE=auto` (local -> remote -> lexical fallback)  
2. Demo day set `EMBEDDING_MODE=local`  
3. Keep separate index per profile (`all-MiniLM-L6-v2-local`, `all-MiniLM-L6-v2-hf`)

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 22.7s)

_Thinking:_



Yes. Enough for v1 and demo.

- `all-MiniLM-L6-v2` works well for English schema/instruction/query-memory retrieval.
- Speed good on 8th-gen i7 CPU; desktop GPU only helps faster indexing.
- 384-dim vectors keep index small, fast.
- Add hybrid retrieval (vector + exact table/column name match). SQL tasks need lexical signal too.
- Keep embedding pipeline consistent: same model, same normalization, same chunking for index/query.
- If multilingual/business jargon heavy, this model weakens. Then move to `BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5` (English) or `BAAI/bge-m3` (multilingual).
- Practical “enough” gate: top-5 context recall >= 85% on your eval set, and end-to-end SQL success improves clearly vs baseline.

- `CONTEXT_PROJECT_ID: str = "default"` project partition key.
- `CONTEXT_STORE_PATH: Path = ROOT_DIR / ".app_cache" / "context_layer.sqlite"` unified local store.
- `CONTEXT_DEBUG_LOG: bool = False` verbose retrieval/index logs.

- `EMBEDDING_MODEL_NAME: str = "sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2"` fixed model profile.
- `EMBEDDING_DEVICE: str = "auto"` desktop cuda, laptop cpu.

- `ENABLE_SCHEMA_CONTEXT: bool = True` schema RAG channel.
- `MDL_DIR: Path = ROOT_DIR / "semantic"` MDL root.
- `MDL_FILE_GLOB: str = "**/*.yaml"` MDL discovery.
- `MDL_BASELINE_FILE: Path = MDL_DIR / "_baseline.generated.yaml"` auto baseline snapshot.
- `MDL_AUTO_GENERATE_BASELINE: bool = True` generate baseline when indexing.
- `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_TOP_K: int = 8` retrieved schema chunks.
- `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MIN_SIMILARITY: float = 0.45` schema threshold.
- `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MAX_CHARS: int = 8000` prompt budget cap.

- `ENABLE_INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT: bool = True` governance channel.
- `INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT_TOP_K: int = 4` retrieved instruction chunks.
- `INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT_MIN_SIMILARITY: float = 0.55` instruction threshold.
- `INSTRUCTION_DEFAULT_FILE: Path = MDL_DIR / "instructions.yaml"` optional static instruction source.

- `ENABLE_QUERY_MEMORY: bool = True` NL->SQL memory channel.
- `QUERY_MEMORY_TOP_K: int = 4` retrieved few-shot examples.
- `QUERY_MEMORY_MIN_SIMILARITY: float = 0.70` few-shot threshold.
- `QUERY_MEMORY_REQUIRE_VERIFIED: bool = True` store only confirmed pairs.
- `QUERY_MEMORY_REQUIRE_NON_EMPTY: bool = True` block empty-result pairs.
- `CLI_ASK_RESULT_CONFIRMATION: bool = True` ask user correctness in CLI.
- `QUERY_MEMORY_MAX_ROWS_PER_DB: int = 5000` memory cap.

- `ENABLE_FAILED_QUERY_LOG: bool = False` off by default to avoid noise.
- `FAILED_QUERY_LOG_MAX_ROWS_PER_DB: int = 20000` bounded failure history.

- `CONTEXT_AUTO_INDEX_ON_STARTUP: bool = False` avoid startup delay.
- `CONTEXT_AUTO_REINDEX_ON_SCHEMA_CHANGE: bool = False` manual first, safer v1.

Existing flags stay unchanged (`EXECUTE_SQL_QUERIES`, `HUMAN_SQL_REVIEW`, `MAX_SQL_RETRIES`, metadata-cache flags).

72:             chunks=all_chunks,
73:         )
74: 
75:         stats = {
76:             "schema_chunks": len(schema_chunks),
77:             "instruction_chunks": len(instruction_chunks),
78:             "total_chunks": inserted,
79:         }
80:         self._store.update_index_state(
81:             project_id=project_id,
82:             db_fingerprint=db_fingerprint,
83:             embedding_model=self.embedding_profile_id,
84:             schema_hash=schema_hash,
85:             stats=stats,
86:         )
87: 
88:         logger.info(
89:             "Context indexed: project=%s db=%s chunks=%s",
90:             project_id,
91:             db_fingerprint,
92:             inserted,
93:         )
94:         return {
95:             "indexed": True,
96:             "project_id": project_id,
97:             "db_fingerprint": db_fingerprint,
98:             "embedding_profile": self.embedding_profile_id,
99:             **stats,
100:         }
101: 
102:     def retrieve_context(self, question: str) -> RetrievedContext:
103:         empty = RetrievedContext(
104:             schema_hits=[],
105:             instruction_hits=[],
106:             query_memory_hits=[],
107:             schema_text="Schema context: none",
108:             instruction_text="Instruction context: none",
109:             query_memory_text="Query memory examples: none",
110:         )
111:         if not self._config.ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER:
112:             return empty
113: 
114:         if not question.strip():
115:             return empty
116: 
117:         query_embedding = self._embedder.embed_query(question)
118:         if not query_embedding:
119:             return empty
120: 
121:         project_id = self._config.CONTEXT_PROJECT_ID
122:         db_fingerprint = get_db_fingerprint()
123: 
124:         schema_hits = (
125:             self._store.search_chunks(
126:                 project_id=project_id,
127:                 db_fingerprint=db_fingerprint,
128:                 embedding_model=self.embedding_profile_id,
129:                 channel="schema",
130:                 query_embedding=query_embedding,
131:                 top_k=self._config.SCHEMA_CONTEXT_TOP_K,
132:                 min_similarity=self._config.SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MIN_SIMILARITY,
133:             )
134:             if self._config.ENABLE_SCHEMA_CONTEXT
135:             else []
136:         )
137: 
138:         instruction_hits = (
139:             self._store.search_chunks(
140:                 project_id=project_id,
141:                 db_fingerprint=db_fingerprint,
142:                 embedding_model=self.embedding_profile_id,
143:                 channel="instructions",
144:                 query_embedding=query_embedding,
145:                 top_k=self._config.INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT_TOP_K,
146:                 min_similarity=self._config.INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT_MIN_SIMILARITY,
147:             )
148:             if self._config.ENABLE_INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT
149:             else []
150:         )
151: 
152:         query_memory_hits = (
153:             self._store.search_query_memory(
154:                 project_id=project_id,
155:                 db_fingerprint=db_fingerprint,
156:                 embedding_model=self.embedding_profile_id,
157:                 query_embedding=query_embedding,
158:                 top_k=self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_TOP_K,
159:                 min_similarity=self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_MIN_SIMILARITY,
160:                 require_verified=self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_REQUIRE_VERIFIED,
161:             )
162:             if self._config.ENABLE_QUERY_MEMORY
163:             else []
164:         )
165: 
166:         schema_text = build_context_text(
167:             hits=schema_hits,
168:             max_chars=self._config.SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MAX_CHARS,
169:             title="Schema context",
170:         )
171:         instruction_text = build_context_text(
172:             hits=instruction_hits,
173:             max_chars=self._config.INSTRUCTION_CONTEXT_MAX_CHARS,
174:             title="Instruction context",
175:         )
176:         query_memory_text = build_query_memory_text(
177:             hits=query_memory_hits,
178:             max_chars=self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_MAX_CHARS,
179:         )
180: 
181:         return RetrievedContext(
182:             schema_hits=schema_hits,
183:             instruction_hits=instruction_hits,
184:             query_memory_hits=query_memory_hits,
185:             schema_text=schema_text,
186:             instruction_text=instruction_text,
187:             query_memory_text=query_memory_text,
188:         )
189: 
190:     def record_verified_query(
191:         self,
192:         *,
193:         question: str,
194:         sql: str,
195:         tables: list[str],
196:         row_count: int,
197:         is_verified: bool,
198:         metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
199:     ) -> str | None:
200:         if (
201:             not self._config.ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER
202:             or not self._config.ENABLE_QUERY_MEMORY
203:         ):
204:             return None
205: 
206:         if self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_REQUIRE_NON_EMPTY and row_count <= 0:
207:             return None
208: 
209:         if self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_REQUIRE_VERIFIED and not is_verified:
210:             return None
211: 
212:         question = question.strip()
213:         sql = sql.strip()
214:         if not question or not sql:
215:             return None
216: 
217:         embedding = self._embedder.embed_query(question)
218:         if not embedding:
219:             return None
220: 
221:         return self._store.upsert_query_memory(
222:             project_id=self._config.CONTEXT_PROJECT_ID,
223:             db_fingerprint=get_db_fingerprint(),
224:             embedding_model=self.embedding_profile_id,
225:             question=question,
226:             sql=sql,
227:             tables=tables,
228:             row_count=row_count,
229:             is_verified=is_verified,
230:             embedding=embedding,
231:             metadata=metadata or {},
232:             max_rows_per_db=self._config.QUERY_MEMORY_MAX_ROWS_PER_DB,
233:         )
234: 
235:     def record_failed_query(
236:         self,
237:         *,
238:         question: str,
239:         sql: str | None,
240:         status: str,
241:         error_message: str | None,
242:         retry_count: int,
243:         metadata: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
244:     ) -> str | None:
245:         if (
246:             not self._config.ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER
247:             or not self._config.ENABLE_FAILED_QUERY_LOG
248:         ):
249:             return None
250: 
251:         return self._store.log_failed_query(
252:             project_id=self._config.CONTEXT_PROJECT_ID,
253:             db_fingerprint=get_db_fingerprint(),
254:             question=question,
255:             sql=sql,
256:             status=status,
257:             error_message=error_message,
258:             retry_count=retry_count,
259:             metadata=metadata or {},
260:             max_rows_per_db=self._config.FAILED_QUERY_LOG_MAX_ROWS_PER_DB,
261:         )
262: 
263:     def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
264:         return self._store.get_stats(
265:             project_id=self._config.CONTEXT_PROJECT_ID,
266:             db_fingerprint=get_db_fingerprint(),
267:         )
268: 
269: 
270: _context_service: ContextLayerService | None = None
271: 
272: 
273: def get_context_service() -> ContextLayerService:
274:     global _context_service
275:     if _context_service is None:
276:         _context_service = ContextLayerService()
277:     return _context_service
278: 
279: 
280: def safe_parse_row_count(db_output: str | None) -> int:
281:     if not db_output:
282:         return 0
283: 
284:     lowered = db_output.lower()
285:     if "0 rows" in lowered:
286:         return 0
287: 
288:     try:
289:         parsed = json.loads(db_output)
290:         if isinstance(parsed, list):
291:             return len(parsed)
292:         if isinstance(parsed, dict):
293:             if isinstance(parsed.get("rows"), list):
294:                 return len(parsed["rows"])
295:             if isinstance(parsed.get("data"), list):
296:                 return len(parsed["data"])
297:     except Exception:
298:         pass
299: 
300:     if "rows returned" in lowered:
301:         chunks = lowered.replace("\n", " ").split()
302:         for idx, token in enumerate(chunks):
303:             if token == "rows" and idx > 0:
304:                 try:
305:                     return int(chunks[idx - 1])
306:                 except Exception:
307:                     continue
308: 
309:     return 1
310: 
311: 
312: def extract_tables_from_sql(sql: str) -> list[str]:
313:     sql = (sql or "").strip()
314:     if not sql:
315:         return []
316: 
317:     try:
318:         import sqlglot
319:         from sqlglot import exp
320:     except Exception:
321:         return []
322: 
323:     try:
324:         parsed = sqlglot.parse_one(sql)
325:     except Exception:
326:         return []
327: 
328:     names: set[str] = set()
329:     for table in parsed.find_all(exp.Table):
330:         name = table.name
331:         if name:
332:             names.add(name)
333: 
334:     return sorted(names)

143:     def replace_chunks(
144:         self,
145:         *,
146:         project_id: str,
147:         db_fingerprint: str,
148:         embedding_model: str,
149:         chunks: list[ChunkRecord],
150:     ) -> int:
151:         if not chunks:
152:             return 0
153: 
154:         channels = sorted({chunk.channel for chunk in chunks})
155:         now = int(time.time())
156: 
157:         placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in channels)
158:         sql_delete = (
159:             "DELETE FROM context_chunks "
160:             "WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ? AND embedding_model = ? "
161:             f"AND channel IN ({placeholders})"
162:         )
163:         delete_args = [project_id, db_fingerprint, embedding_model, *channels]
164: 
165:         rows = [
166:             (
167:                 chunk.id,
168:                 project_id,
169:                 db_fingerprint,
170:                 embedding_model,
171:                 chunk.channel,
172:                 chunk.source_type,
173:                 chunk.name,
174:                 chunk.content,
175:                 to_json(chunk.embedding),
176:                 to_json(chunk.metadata),
177:                 now,
178:             )
179:             for chunk in chunks
180:         ]
181: 
182:         with self._lock:
183:             with self._connect() as conn:
184:                 conn.execute(sql_delete, delete_args)
185:                 conn.executemany(
186:                     """

188:                         id, project_id, db_fingerprint, embedding_model, channel,
189:                         source_type, name, content, embedding_json, meta_json, created_at
190:                     ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
191:                     """,
192:                     rows,
193:                 )
194: 
195:         return len(rows)

197:     def search_chunks(
198:         self,
199:         *,
200:         project_id: str,
201:         db_fingerprint: str,
202:         embedding_model: str,
203:         channel: str,
204:         query_embedding: list[float],
205:         top_k: int,
206:         min_similarity: float,
207:     ) -> list[SearchHit]:
208:         with self._lock:
209:             with self._connect() as conn:
210:                 rows = conn.execute(
211:                     """
212:                     SELECT id, name, source_type, content, embedding_json, meta_json
213:                     FROM context_chunks
214:                     WHERE project_id = ?
215:                       AND db_fingerprint = ?
216:                       AND embedding_model = ?
217:                       AND channel = ?
218:                     """,
219:                     (project_id, db_fingerprint, embedding_model, channel),
220:                 ).fetchall()
221: 
222:         scored: list[SearchHit] = []
223:         for row in rows:
224:             record_id, name, source_type, content, embedding_json, meta_json = row
225:             embedding = from_json(embedding_json, default=[])
226:             score = cosine_similarity(query_embedding, embedding)
227:             if score < min_similarity:

229:             scored.append(
230:                 SearchHit(

234:                     content=content,
235:                     score=score,
236:                     metadata=from_json(meta_json, default={}),
237:                 )
238:             )
239: 
240:         scored.sort(key=lambda hit: hit.score, reverse=True)
241:         return scored[:top_k]

459:         project_id: str,
460:         db_fingerprint: str,
461:         embedding_model: str,
462:     ) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
463:         with self._lock:
464:             with self._connect() as conn:
465:                 row = conn.execute(
466:                     """
467:                     SELECT schema_hash, stats_json, indexed_at
468:                     FROM context_index_state
469:                     WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ? AND embedding_model = ?
470:                     """,
471:                     (project_id, db_fingerprint, embedding_model),
472:                 ).fetchone()
473:         if row is None:
474:             return None
475:         schema_hash, stats_json, indexed_at = row
476:         return {
477:             "schema_hash": schema_hash,
478:             "stats": from_json(stats_json, default={}),
479:             "indexed_at": indexed_at,
480:         }
481: 
482:     def get_stats(self, *, project_id: str, db_fingerprint: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
483:         with self._lock:
484:             with self._connect() as conn:
485:                 chunk_counts = conn.execute(
486:                     """
487:                     SELECT channel, COUNT(*)
488:                     FROM context_chunks
489:                     WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ?
490:                     GROUP BY channel
491:                     """,
492:                     (project_id, db_fingerprint),
493:                 ).fetchall()
494: 
495:                 query_memory_count = conn.execute(
496:                     """
497:                     SELECT COUNT(*)
498:                     FROM query_memory
499:                     WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ?
500:                     """,
501:                     (project_id, db_fingerprint),
502:                 ).fetchone()[0]
503: 
504:                 failed_count = conn.execute(
505:                     """
506:                     SELECT COUNT(*)
507:                     FROM failed_queries
508:                     WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ?
509:                     """,
510:                     (project_id, db_fingerprint),
511:                 ).fetchone()[0]
512: 
513:         return {
514:             "chunks": {channel: count for channel, count in chunk_counts},
515:             "query_memory": int(query_memory_count),
516:             "failed_queries": int(failed_count),
517:         }
518: 
519:     def _trim_rows(
520:         self,
521:         conn: sqlite3.Connection,
522:         *,
523:         table: str,
524:         project_id: str,
525:         db_fingerprint: str,
526:         max_rows: int,
527:     ) -> None:
528:         if max_rows <= 0:
529:             return
530: 
531:         count = conn.execute(
532:             f"""
533:             SELECT COUNT(*)
534:             FROM {table}
535:             WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ?
536:             """,
537:             (project_id, db_fingerprint),
538:         ).fetchone()[0]
539: 
540:         over = int(count) - max_rows
541:         if over <= 0:
542:             return
543: 
544:         conn.execute(
545:             f"""
546:             DELETE FROM {table}
547:             WHERE id IN (
548:                 SELECT id
549:                 FROM {table}
550:                 WHERE project_id = ? AND db_fingerprint = ?
551:                 ORDER BY created_at ASC
552:                 LIMIT ?
553:             )
554:             """,
555:             (project_id, db_fingerprint, over),
556:         )

16: def load_yaml_documents(root_dir: Path, file_glob: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
17:     try:
18:         import yaml
19:     except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - import guard
20:         raise RuntimeError("Missing dependency 'pyyaml'.") from exc
21: 
22:     if not root_dir.exists():
23:         return []
24:

26:     for path in sorted(root_dir.glob(file_glob)):

28:             continue
29:         if not path.is_file():
30:             continue
31: 
32:         loaded = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
33:         if isinstance(loaded, dict):
34:             docs.append(loaded)
35:     return docs

38: def build_baseline_semantic_model(database, table_names: list[str]) -> SemanticModel:
39:     models: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
40:     relationships: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
41: 
42:     inspector = _build_inspector(database)
43: 
44:     for table in table_names:
45:         columns = []
46:         for col in inspector.get_columns(table):
47:             columns.append(
48:                 {
49:                     "name": col.get("name"),
50:                     "type": str(col.get("type", "")),
51:                     "nullable": bool(col.get("nullable", True)),
52:                     "primary_key": bool(col.get("primary_key", False)),
53:                     "description": "",
54:                 }
55:             )
56: 
57:         models.append(
58:             {
59:                 "name": table,
60:                 "table": table,
61:                 "description": "",
62:                 "aliases": [],
63:                 "columns": columns,
64:             }
65:         )
66: 
67:         for fk in inspector.get_foreign_keys(table):
68:             constrained_columns = fk.get("constrained_columns") or []
69:             referred_columns = fk.get("referred_columns") or []
70:             referred_table = fk.get("referred_table")
71:             if not constrained_columns or not referred_columns or not referred_table:
72:                 continue
73: 
74:             relationships.append(
75:                 {
76:                     "from_model": table,
77:                     "to_model": referred_table,
78:                     "type": "many_to_one",
79:                     "join": {
80:                         "from_column": constrained_columns[0],
81:                         "to_column": referred_columns[0],
82:                     },
83:                     "description": "",
84:                 }
85:             )
86: 
87:     return SemanticModel(models=models, relationships=relationships, instructions=[])
88: 
89: 
90: def merge_semantic_model(
91:     baseline: SemanticModel,
92:     docs: list[dict[str, Any]],
93: ) -> SemanticModel:
94:     model_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
95:         model["name"]: json.loads(json.dumps(model)) for model in baseline.models
96:     }
97:     relationships = list(baseline.relationships)
98:     instructions = list(baseline.instructions)
99: 
100:     for doc in docs:
101:         for model in doc.get("models", []):
102:             name = model.get("name")
103:             if not name:
104:                 continue
105: 
106:             if name not in model_map:
107:                 model_map[name] = {
108:                     "name": name,
109:                     "table": model.get("table") or name,
110:                     "description": model.get("description") or "",
111:                     "aliases": model.get("aliases") or [],
112:                     "columns": model.get("columns") or [],
113:                 }
114:                 continue
115: 
116:             target = model_map[name]
117:             for field in ["description", "aliases", "table"]:
118:                 if field in model and model[field] is not None:
119:                     target[field] = model[field]
120: 
121:             if "columns" in model and isinstance(model["columns"], list):
122:                 target_columns = {
123:                     c.get("name"): c
124:                     for c in target.get("columns", [])
125:                     if isinstance(c, dict) and c.get("name")
126:                 }
127:                 for col in model["columns"]:
128:                     col_name = col.get("name") if isinstance(col, dict) else None
129:                     if not col_name:
130:                         continue
131:                     if col_name not in target_columns:
132:                         target.get("columns", []).append(col)
133:                         target_columns[col_name] = col
134:                     else:
135:                         merged = target_columns[col_name]
136:                         for k, v in col.items():
137:                             if v is not None:
138:                                 merged[k] = v
139: 
140:         relationships.extend(doc.get("relationships", []))
141:         instructions.extend(doc.get("instructions", []))
142: 
143:     return SemanticModel(
144:         models=list(model_map.values()),
145:         relationships=relationships,
146:         instructions=instructions,
147:     )
148: 
149: 
150: def dump_baseline_yaml(baseline: SemanticModel, output_path: Path) -> None:
151:     try:
152:         import yaml
153:     except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
154:         raise RuntimeError("Missing dependency 'pyyaml'.") from exc
155: 
156:     payload = {
157:         "models": baseline.models,
158:         "relationships": baseline.relationships,
159:         "instructions": baseline.instructions,
160:     }
161: 
162:     output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
163:     output_path.write_text(yaml.safe_dump(payload, sort_keys=False), encoding="utf-8")
164: 
165: 
166: def load_or_build_semantic_model(database, table_names: list[str]) -> SemanticModel:
167:     config = AppConfig()
168:     baseline = build_baseline_semantic_model(database, table_names)
169: 
170:     if config.MDL_AUTO_GENERATE_BASELINE:
171:         dump_baseline_yaml(baseline, config.MDL_BASELINE_FILE)
172: 
173:     docs = load_yaml_documents(config.MDL_DIR, config.MDL_FILE_GLOB)
174:     if not docs:
175:         return baseline
176: 
177:     return merge_semantic_model(baseline, docs)
178: 
179: 
180: def _build_inspector(database):
181:     try:
182:         from sqlalchemy import inspect
183:     except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
184:         raise RuntimeError("Missing dependency 'sqlalchemy'.") from exc
185: 
186:     engine = getattr(database, "_engine", None) or getattr(database, "engine", None)
187:     if engine is None:
188:         raise RuntimeError(
189:             "Could not access SQLAlchemy engine from SQLDatabase for schema introspection."
190:         )
191:     return inspect(engine)

106:     )
107:     query_memory_context = (
108:         state.get("query_memory_context") or "Query memory examples: none"
109:     )
110: 
111:     system_message = {
112:         "role": "system",
113:         "content": GENERATE_QUERY.format(
114:             dialect=db.dialect,
115:             top_k=5,
116:             schema_context=schema_context,
117:             instruction_context=instruction_context,
118:             query_memory_context=query_memory_context,
119:         ),
120:     }
121:     llm_with_tools = model.bind_tools(
122:         [run_query_tool_with_interrupt], tool_choice="any"
123:     )
124: 
125:     if state["analysis_result"] is not None and state["analysis_result"].status in [
126:         "error",
127:         "empty_result",
128:     ]:
129:         original_messages = [m for m in state["messages"] if m.type == "human"]
130:         user_message = {
131:             "role": "user",
132:             "content": REGEN_PROMPTS[state["analysis_result"].status].format(
133:                 query=state["last_query"],
134:                 explanation=state["analysis_result"].explanation,
135:             ),
136:         }
137: 
138:         response = llm_with_tools.invoke(
139:             [system_message] + original_messages + [user_message]
140:         )
141: 
142:         current_retry_count = state["retry_count"] + 1
143:         return {"messages": [response], "retry_count": current_retry_count}
144: 
145:     response = llm_with_tools.invoke([system_message] + state["messages"])
146:     logger.debug(f"Generated Query: {response}")
147: 
148:     return {"messages": [response]}
149: 
150: 
151: def retrieve_context(state: AgentState):
152:     if not AppConfig().ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER:
153:         return {
154:             "schema_context": "Schema context: none",
155:             "instruction_context": "Instruction context: none",
156:             "query_memory_context": "Query memory examples: none",
157:         }
158: 
159:     service = get_context_service()
160:     result = service.retrieve_context(state["user_question"])
161:     return {
162:         "schema_context": result.schema_text,
163:         "instruction_context": result.instruction_text,
164:         "query_memory_context": result.query_memory_text,
165:     }
166: 
167: 
168: def check_query(state: AgentState):
169:     system_message = {
170:         "role": "system",
171:         "content": CHECK_QUERY.format(dialect=db.dialect),
172:     }
173: 
174:     tool_call = state["messages"][-1].tool_calls[0]
175:     user_message = {"role": "user", "content": tool_call["args"]["query"]}
176:     llm_with_tools = model.bind_tools(
177:         [run_query_tool_with_interrupt], tool_choice="any"
178:     )
179:     response = llm_with_tools.invoke([system_message, user_message])
180:     response.id = state["messages"][-1].id
181:     last_query = response.tool_calls[0]["args"]["query"]
182: 
183:     logger.debug(f"Check Query Response: {response}")
184:     logger.debug(f"Checked Query: {last_query}")
185: 
186:     return {
187:         "messages": [response],
188:         "last_query": last_query,
189:         "previous_queries": [last_query],
190:     }
191: 
192: 
193: def should_execute(_state: AgentState):
194:     if not AppConfig().EXECUTE_SQL_QUERIES:
195:         return "explain_result"
196:     return "run_query"
197: 
198: 
199: run_query_node = ToolNode([run_query_tool_with_interrupt], name="run_query")
200: 
201: 
202: def analyze_result(state: AgentState):
203:     db_output = state["messages"][-1].content or "Empty, 0 rows returned"
204: 
205:     system_message = {
206:         "role": "system",
207:         "content": ANALYZE_RESULT.format(
208:             user_input=state["user_question"],
209:             query_executed=state["last_query"],
210:             database_output=db_output,
211:             retry_count=state["retry_count"],
212:         ),
213:     }
214:     structured_llm = model.with_structured_output(AnalysisResult)
215: 
216:     response = structured_llm.invoke([system_message])
217: 
218:     logger.debug(f"Analysis Result: {response.model_dump_json()}")
219: 
220:     return {
221:         "db_output": db_output,
222:         "analysis_result": response,
223:     }
224: 
225: 
226: def should_retry(state: AgentState):
227:     analysis_result = state.get("analysis_result")
228: 
229:     if state["retry_count"] < AppConfig().MAX_SQL_RETRIES:
230:         if analysis_result.status == "irrelevant":
231:             return "call_get_schema"
232:         if analysis_result.status in ["error", "empty_result"]:
233:             return "generate_query"
234:         # explicit exit to prevent repeated queries
235:         if (
236:             len(state["previous_queries"]) > 1
237:             and state["last_query"] in state["previous_queries"][:-1]
238:         ):
239:             return "explain_result"
240: 
241:     return "explain_result"
242: 
243: 
244: def explain_result(state: AgentState):
245:     if not AppConfig().EXECUTE_SQL_QUERIES:
246:         explanation = f"SQL execution is disabled. Skipping query . Here is last generated query: {state['last_query']}"
247:         logger.debug(f"No Execution:{explanation}")
248:         return {"messages": [AIMessage(content=explanation)]}
249: 
250:     if state["skip_decision"] and state["skip_decision"].skip:
251:         explanation = f"The agent decided to skip executing the query because: {state['skip_decision'].reason}"
252:         logger.debug(f"Skip Explanation: {explanation}")
253:         return {"messages": [AIMessage(content=explanation)]}
254: 
255:     system_message = {
256:         "role": "system",
257:         "content": EXPLAIN_RESULT.format(
258:             user_question=state["user_question"],
259:             query=state["last_query"],
260:             status=state["analysis_result"].status,
261:             database_output=state["db_output"],
262:             explanation=state["analysis_result"].explanation,
263:         ),
264:     }
265:     response = model.invoke([system_message] + state["messages"])
266:     logger.debug(f"Explanation: {response}")
267:     return {"messages": [response]}

+@mcp.tool()
+async def context_reindex() -> str:
+    """
+    Rebuild semantic context index from live schema and MDL YAML files.
+    """
+    from config.app_config import AppConfig
+
+    if not AppConfig().ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER:
+        return "Context layer disabled in AppConfig."
+
+    service = get_context_service()
+    table_names = list(db.get_usable_table_names())
+    summary = service.index_semantic_context(db, table_names)
+    return f"Context reindex done: {summary}"
+
+
+@mcp.tool()
+async def context_stats() -> str:
+    """
+    Return context layer stats for active project/database.
+    """
+    from config.app_config import AppConfig
+
+    if not AppConfig().ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER:
+        return "Context layer disabled in AppConfig."
+
+    return str(get_context_service().get_stats())
+
+
+@mcp.tool()
+async def confirm_sql_pair(question: str, sql: str, row_count: int = 1) -> str:
+    """
+    Persist a verified NL->SQL pair into query memory.
+    """
+    from config.app_config import AppConfig
+
+    config = AppConfig()
+    if not config.ENABLE_CONTEXT_LAYER or not config.ENABLE_QUERY_MEMORY:
+        return "Query memory disabled in AppConfig."
+
+    memory_id = get_context_service().record_verified_query(
+        question=question,
+        sql=sql,
+        tables=extract_tables_from_sql(sql),
+        row_count=max(0, int(row_count)),
+        is_verified=True,
+        metadata={"source": "mcp", "confirmed": True},
+    )
+    if not memory_id:
+        return "Pair did not meet storage policy (check verification/non-empty rules)."
+    return f"Stored verified pair id: {memory_id}"
+
+
 if __name__ == "__main__":
     mcp.run(transport="streamable-http")

 while im waiting for uv sync to download, explain to me what you did in the src/context_layer  and explain each file what it does

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 39.2s)

_Thinking:_



Good timing. Here full map of `src/context_layer`.

**What `context_layer` does**
- Builds sidecar semantic context engine. Goal: feed agent 3 channels before SQL gen: schema context, query memory, instructions.
- Runs in 3 phases: index-time, query-time, learn-time.
- Keeps old flow intact. If context flags off, agent behaves like before.

**Index-time flow**
- Read live DB schema via SQLAlchemy inspector.
- Read YAML overrides from `semantic/*.yaml`.
- Merge baseline + overrides into one semantic model.
- Chunk model into retrievable docs (`schema`, `instructions`).
- Embed chunks with local ST model.
- Store vectors + metadata in local SQLite (`.app_cache/context_layer.sqlite`).

**Query-time flow**
- Embed user question once.
- Retrieve top-k hits per channel by cosine similarity.
- Build prompt-safe text blocks with char budgets.
- Inject blocks into `GENERATE_QUERY` prompt.

**Learn-time flow**
- After successful run + user confirms correct in CLI, save verified NL->SQL pair.
- Pair gets embedded and stored in query memory channel.
- Optional failed-query logging exists, flag-gated, separate table.

---

**File-by-file**

- `src/context_layer/__init__.py`  
  - Exposes `get_context_service()` singleton. Simple import surface.

- `src/context_layer/common.py`  
  - Shared utilities: hashing, db fingerprint, JSON encode/decode, whitespace normalize, cosine similarity.
  - `get_db_fingerprint()` hashes active DB URI, so memory/context stays scoped per DB.

- `src/context_layer/store.py`  
  - SQLite persistence layer.
  - Creates tables:
    - `context_chunks` (schema/instruction vector docs)
    - `query_memory` (verified NL->SQL pairs + question embeddings)
    - `failed_queries` (optional diagnostics)
    - `context_index_state` (last index hash/stats)
  - Core methods:
    - `replace_chunks(...)` delete+upsert by channel/profile.
    - `search_chunks(...)` linear cosine scan + top-k filter.
    - `upsert_query_memory(...)` dedupe on normalized `(question, sql)`.
    - `search_query_memory(...)` retrieves few-shot examples.
    - `log_failed_query(...)` stores failures.
    - `get_stats(...)` quick counts.
  - Note: similarity search now brute-force in Python (fine for MVP scale).

- `src/context_layer/embedder.py`  
  - Local SentenceTransformer wrapper.
  - Uses AppConfig model/device/normalize/batch flags.
  - Exposes:
    - `embed_texts(...)` for indexing
    - `embed_query(...)` for runtime retrieval
  - Embedding profile id format: `local:<model-name>`.

- `src/context_layer/md_loader.py`  
  - Semantic model builder.
  - `build_baseline_semantic_model(...)` introspects tables/columns/FKs from live DB.
  - `load_yaml_documents(...)` loads MDL-lite YAML files from `semantic/`, skips `_baseline.generated.yaml`.
  - `merge_semantic_model(...)` applies YAML overrides (descriptions, aliases, columns, relationships, instructions).
  - `dump_baseline_yaml(...)` writes generated baseline YAML for editing.
  - Handles `schema.table` names with `_split_table_identifier(...)`.

- `src/context_layer/chunking.py`  
  - Converts semantic model into chunk docs.
  - `build_schema_chunks(...)`: model summaries, column lists, relationships.
  - `build_instruction_chunks(...)`: governance/instruction chunks.
  - `build_context_text(...)` and `build_query_memory_text(...)`: format retrieved hits into prompt blocks with char budget caps.

- `src/context_layer/service.py`  
  - Orchestrator. Main public API.
  - `index_semantic_context(...)`:
    - load/merge semantic model
    - chunk + embed
    - persist schema/instruction channels
    - update index state hash/stats
  - `retrieve_context(question)`:
    - embed question
    - retrieve per-channel with thresholds/top-k from AppConfig
    - return both raw hits + prompt-ready text
  - `record_verified_query(...)`: saves confirmed NL->SQL pair (policy checks enforced).
  - `record_failed_query(...)`: saves failed events when enabled.
  - `get_stats()`: returns counts.
  - Helpers:
    - `safe_parse_row_count(...)` tries to infer row count from tool output.
    - `extract_tables_from_sql(...)` parses table names via `sqlglot`.

---

**Also added around it**
- `semantic/README.md`: MDL-lite format docs + example YAML.
- Agent now calls context retrieval node before SQL gen.
- CLI has `--context-reindex` and `--context-stats`.
- CLI asks confirmation after success, then stores verified pair.

If you want next, I map one concrete question through full runtime path (step-by-step with real function calls).

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 37.6s)

_Thinking:_



Clear sequence mode for this one (avoid misread). Then caveman resume.

**Concrete Runtime Path**

Example question: `Which 5 artists generated most revenue?`

- `src/main.py` builds `initial_state` and runs graph stream.
- Graph route in `src/agent/graph.py`:
  - `list_tables` -> `skip_pipeline` -> `call_get_schema` -> `get_schema` -> `retrieve_context` -> `generate_query` -> `check_query` -> `run_query` -> `analyze_result` -> `explain_result`.

**What happens at `retrieve_context`**
- `src/agent/nodes.py::retrieve_context` calls `get_context_service().retrieve_context(user_question)`.
- `src/context_layer/service.py::retrieve_context`:
  - embeds question once with local ST model (`all-MiniLM-L6-v2`) via `src/context_layer/embedder.py`.
  - computes `project_id` + `db_fingerprint`.
  - retrieves top-k schema hits from `context_chunks` channel `schema`.
  - retrieves top-k instruction hits from `context_chunks` channel `instructions`.
  - retrieves top-k verified NL->SQL hits from `query_memory`.
  - builds 3 prompt blocks:
    - `schema_text`
    - `instruction_text`
    - `query_memory_text`
- If nothing indexed yet, blocks become `...: none` and flow still works.

**How SQL generation uses it**
- `src/agent/prompts.py::GENERATE_QUERY` now has 3 sections:
  - `[SCHEMA CONTEXT]`
  - `[QUERY MEMORY]`
  - `[INSTRUCTION CONTEXT]`
- `src/agent/nodes.py::generate_query` injects these fields into system prompt before LLM tool call.

**After SQL executes**
- `analyze_result` classifies `success | empty_result | irrelevant | error`.
- Retry logic unchanged (same guardrails).
- `explain_result` returns user-facing answer.

**Learning loop (CLI)**
- In `src/main.py`, after final state:
  - if status = `success`, CLI asks: `Was result correct? [y/N]`.
  - if yes: `record_verified_query(...)` stores pair in `query_memory`.
- Stored data includes:
  - question, SQL, parsed table names, row_count, verification flag, embedding, metadata.
- Table used: `query_memory` in `.app_cache/context_layer.sqlite`.

**Failed request logging (optional)**
- If `ENABLE_FAILED_QUERY_LOG=True`, non-success final states go into `failed_queries`.
- Never used as few-shot examples.

**Indexing path (separate command)**
- `python src/main.py --context-reindex`
- Calls `index_semantic_context(...)` in `src/context_layer/service.py`:
  - baseline from live DB introspection (`src/context_layer/md_loader.py`)
  - merge YAML overrides from `semantic/*.yaml`
  - chunk (`src/context_layer/chunking.py`)
  - embed
  - replace channels in `context_chunks`
  - save index stats/hash in `context_index_state`

**Storage schema**
- All in `src/context_layer/store.py` SQLite DB:
  - `context_chunks` (schema/instructions)
  - `query_memory` (verified NL->SQL)
  - `failed_queries` (optional diagnostics)
  - `context_index_state` (index metadata)

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 18.9s)

_Thinking:_



Yes. Schema context = **schema slice**, not blind full dump.

- Raw schema = all tables/columns/FKs from DB.
- Schema context = retrieved chunks from that schema + MDL hints, only parts relevant to question.
- In your impl now, chunks come from `model summary`, `model columns`, `relationships` in `src/context_layer/chunking.py`.
- Retrieval step in `src/context_layer/service.py`: embed question -> top-k similar schema chunks -> pass to prompt.
- Size controlled by `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_TOP_K`, `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MIN_SIMILARITY`, `SCHEMA_CONTEXT_MAX_CHARS` in `src/config/app_config.py`.
- So answer: yes, table schema included, but filtered/chunked first. Not full schema dump every run.

One design choice to lock:  
If retrieval finds weak/no hits, fallback to full schema of candidate tables, or keep `schema context: none`? Recommended: fallback to candidate-table full schema.

## Assistant (Plan · GPT-5.3-Codex · 19.2s)

_Thinking:_



Yes. Query memory save unique keys now.

- `src/context_layer/store.py` unique index = (`project_id`, `db_fingerprint`, `question_norm`, `sql`).
- Same normalized question + same SQL -> `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE`, not new row.
- Verified gate active from CLI flow (`y/yes`), plus non-empty row check.
- Caveat: SQL text diff (format/alias/order) counts as different. Semantic duplicate still pass. Later fix with SQL canonicalization (`sqlglot` normalize).

You right on schema logic. Current flow duplicates context.

- `call_get_schema` already returns full schema for selected tables.
- New `retrieve_context` also injects schema chunks.
- Result = token bloat, repeated info.

Best revisit plan (recommended):

1. Question -> `retrieve_context` first.  
2. Build candidate tables from schema hits (+ existing table-name logic union).  
3. Call `get_schema` only for narrowed candidate set.  
4. Prompt gets: compact schema context + full schema for narrowed tables only.  
5. If retrieval weak, fallback old behavior.

One lock choice:
- Keep full `get_schema` always (safer, more tokens), or
- Conditional `get_schema` only when retrieval confidence low (recommended)?
